- **registry-publish**: Maintainer-side command. Takes a directory containing a `vibe.toml` with a `[package]` table and publishes it as a tagged release in the configured registry's organization. Creates the per-package repo (or reuses an existing one), pushes contents, tags the version. Mechanical only — no semantic / LLM-backed review (that's reserved for v2+ per [`VIBEVM-SPEC.md` §8.5](../../VIBEVM-SPEC.md)). ([vibevm/vibevm](https://github.com/vibevm/vibevm/blob/HEAD/docs-legacy/commands/registry-publish.md) · ★91)
- **registry-recommend**: [&larr; Command reference](index.md) · [`lean-pkg registry`](registry.md) · [CLI overview](../cli.md) ([lean-pkg/lean-pkg](https://github.com/lean-pkg/lean-pkg/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/registry-recommend.md) · ★4)
- **registry-redirect**: Maintainer-side command. Creates a registry stub repo carrying `vibe-redirect.toml` instead of package content; consumers reach the package transparently through the stub via `vibe install &lt;pkgref&gt;`. Per PROP-002 §2.4.2; see [`docs/registry-redirect.md`](../registry-redirect.md) for the full operator reference. ([vibevm/vibevm](https://github.com/vibevm/vibevm/blob/HEAD/docs-legacy/commands/registry-redirect.md) · ★91)
- **registry-redirect-sync**: Maintainer-side command. Reads an existing registry stub's `vibe-redirect.toml`, enumerates target-side tags, and pushes the missing ones into the stub. Per PROP-002 §2.4.2 `pass-through-tag` policy: the stub's tags determine which target versions the org's namespace exposes; this command keeps them in sync. ([vibevm/vibevm](https://github.com/vibevm/vibevm/blob/HEAD/docs-legacy/commands/registry-redirect-sync.md) · ★91)
- **registry-redirect-update**: Maintainer-side command. Mutates the `vibe-redirect.toml` of an existing stub repo in-place: each flag is optional, so the command does a true partial update — fields not passed retain their current value. Closes the [v0 manual-procedure gap](../registry-redirect.md#manual-procedure-fallback) where retargeting a stub required a hand-driven `git clone` / edit / push. ([vibevm/vibevm](https://github.com/vibevm/vibevm/blob/HEAD/docs-legacy/commands/registry-redirect-update.md) · ★91)
- **registry-remove**: Mutates `vibe.toml` to remove either a registry block (by name) or a mirror block (by exact `(of, url)` match). The two targets are spelled as subsubcommands so the CLI surface is unambiguous and shell completion works cleanly: ([vibevm/vibevm](https://github.com/vibevm/vibevm/blob/HEAD/docs-legacy/commands/registry-remove.md) · ★91)
- **registry-review**: Review registry changes for quality before committing (checks code, YAML, CI, and infrastructure). ([pulumi/registry](https://github.com/pulumi/registry/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/registry-review.md) · ★43)
- **registry-scaffold**: {% alert type="note" title="Description" %} Scaffold a Xano registry folder with a sample component. A registry has three parts: • index.json — the item list (each entry follows the registry item schema) • &lt;type&gt;/&lt;name&gt;.json — item descriptors (e.g. functions/hello-world.json) • components/&lt;type&gt;/&lt;name&gt;.xs — the actual source files that items point at An item's files[].path points into components/ ([calycode/xano-tools](https://github.com/calycode/xano-tools/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/registry-scaffold.md))
- **registry-scan**: Discover and index all components in .claude/ directory ([nathanvale/vtm-cli](https://github.com/nathanvale/vtm-cli/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/registry-scan.md))

- **registry-set-mirror**: Mutates `vibe.toml` to add a new `[[mirror]]` block. A mirror is a transparent alternative URL for a registry: when the primary URL fails (or by priority order), the resolver falls through to the mirror with no change to the lockfile or to per-package identity. `vibe registry set-mirror` is the manifest-side configuration; runtime mirror dispatch lands in M1.6 Phase B. ([vibevm/vibevm](https://github.com/vibevm/vibevm/blob/HEAD/docs-legacy/commands/registry-set-mirror.md) · ★91)
- **registry-sync**: Walks the project's `vibe.lock` and refreshes the on-disk clone of every package referenced by it — the per-package model's equivalent of `git fetch` against a single monorepo. Useful before a `vibe install` of a new version when you want the freshest tag list, and as a cache-warming step in CI. ([vibevm/vibevm](https://github.com/vibevm/vibevm/blob/HEAD/docs-legacy/commands/registry-sync.md) · ★91)

- **release**: - **Version matching**: The release version must match the `release` value in your `Sentry.init()` call. If your SDK uses `"1.0.0"`, create the release as `sentry release create org/1.0.0` (version = `1.0.0`), **not** `sentry release create org/myapp/1.0.0`. - **The `org/` prefix is the org slug**: In `sentry release create sentry/1.0.0`, `sentry` is the org slug and `1.0.0` is the version.
